FILE: combine_context.py
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
     1 | from __future__ import annotations
     2 | 
     3 | import argparse
     4 | from pathlib import Path
     5 | 
     6 | GENERATED_DIRECTORIES = {
     7 |     ".venv",
     8 |     "__pycache__",
     9 |     "dist",
    10 |     "node_modules",
    11 |     "venv",
    12 | }
    13 | ALWAYS_EXCLUDED_DIRECTORIES = GENERATED_DIRECTORIES | {".git"}
    14 | 
    15 | 
    16 | def is_binary(path: Path) -> bool:
    17 |     with path.open("rb") as file:
    18 |         sample = file.read(8192)
    19 | 
    20 |     if b"\0" in sample:
    21 |         return True
    22 | 
    23 |     text = sample.decode("utf-8", errors="ignore")
    24 |     control_characters = sum(
    25 |         ord(character) < 32 and character not in "\n\r\t\f\b"
    26 |         for character in text
    27 |     )
    28 |     return len(text) > 0 and control_characters / len(text) > 0.30
    29 | 
    30 | 
    31 | def collect_files(root: Path, output: Path, include_generated: bool) -> list[Path]:
    32 |     excluded_directories = (
    33 |         {".git"} if include_generated else ALWAYS_EXCLUDED_DIRECTORIES
    34 |     )
    35 |     files: list[Path] = []
    36 | 
    37 |     for path in root.rglob("*"):
    38 |         if not path.is_file():
    39 |             continue
    40 | 
    41 |         relative_path = path.relative_to(root)
    42 |         if path.resolve() == output.resolve():
    43 |             continue
    44 |         if excluded_directories.intersection(relative_path.parts):
    45 |             continue
    46 | 
    47 |         files.append(path)
    48 | 
    49 |     return sorted(files, key=lambda path: path.relative_to(root).as_posix().lower())
    50 | 
    51 | 
    52 | def combine_files(root: Path, output: Path, include_generated: bool) -> tuple[int, int]:
    53 |     files = collect_files(root, output, include_generated)
    54 |     binary_files = 0
    55 | 
    56 |     with output.open("w", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n") as context_file:
    57 |         context_file.write("AI PROJECT CONTEXT\n")
    58 |         context_file.write(f"Source directory: {root.resolve()}\n")
    59 |         context_file.write(f"Files included: {len(files)}\n\n")
    60 | 
    61 |         for path in files:
    62 |             relative_path = path.relative_to(root).as_posix()
    63 |             context_file.write("=" * 80)
    64 |             context_file.write("\n")
    65 |             context_file.write(f"FILE: {relative_path}\n")
    66 |             context_file.write("-" * 80)
    67 |             context_file.write("\n")
    68 | 
    69 |             if is_binary(path):
    70 |                 context_file.write("[Binary file omitted]\n\n")
    71 |                 binary_files += 1
    72 |                 continue
    73 | 
    74 |             content = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    75 |             for line_number, line in enumerate(content.splitlines(), start=1):
    76 |                 context_file.write(f"{line_number:6d} | {line}\n")
    77 |             context_file.write("\n")
    78 | 
    79 |     return len(files), binary_files
    80 | 
    81 | 
    82 | def parse_arguments() -> argparse.Namespace:
    83 |     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
    84 |         description="Combine project files into one line-numbered context file."
    85 |     )
    86 |     parser.add_argument(
    87 |         "directory",
    88 |         nargs="?",
    89 |         type=Path,
    90 |         default=Path.cwd(),
    91 |         help="Directory to scan (default: current directory).",
    92 |     )
    93 |     parser.add_argument(
    94 |         "output",
    95 |         nargs="?",
    96 |         type=Path,
    97 |         default=Path("context.txt"),
    98 |         help="Output text file (default: context.txt).",
    99 |     )
   100 |     parser.add_argument(
   101 |         "--include-generated",
   102 |         action="store_true",
   103 |         help="Include node_modules, dist, virtual environments, and caches.",
   104 |     )
   105 |     return parser.parse_args()
   106 | 
   107 | 
   108 | def main() -> None:
   109 |     arguments = parse_arguments()
   110 |     root = arguments.directory.resolve()
   111 |     output = arguments.output.resolve()
   112 | 
   113 |     if not root.is_dir():
   114 |         raise SystemExit(f"Directory does not exist: {root}")
   115 | 
   116 |     output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   117 |     file_count, binary_count = combine_files(
   118 |         root,
   119 |         output,
   120 |         arguments.include_generated,
   121 |     )
   122 | 
   123 |     print(f"Wrote {output} with {file_count} files.")
   124 |     print(f"Omitted {binary_count} binary files.")
   125 | 
   126 | 
   127 | if __name__ == "__main__":
   128 |     main()

     1 | import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
     2 | import { useFavorites } from '../context/FavoritesContext';
     3 | import type { Artwork } from '../types';
     4 | import { getArtist } from '../data/museumData';
     5 | 
     6 | export function ArtworkCard({ artwork, variant = 'standard' }: { artwork: Artwork; variant?: 'standard' | 'feature' | 'compact' }) {
     7 |   const artist = getArtist(artwork.artistId);
     8 |   const { isFavorite, toggleFavorite } = useFavorites();
     9 |   const favorite = isFavorite(artwork.id);
    10 | 
    11 |   return (
    12 |     <article className={`artwork-card artwork-card-${variant}`}>
    13 |       <Link className="artwork-card-image" to={`/works/${artwork.id}`} aria-label={`View ${artwork.title}`}>
    14 |         <img src={artwork.image} alt={artwork.title} loading={variant === 'feature' ? 'eager' : 'lazy'} />
    15 |         {artwork.featured && <span className="image-flag">Featured</span>}
    16 |       </Link>
    17 |       <div className="artwork-card-body">
    18 |         <div className="artwork-card-topline">
    19 |           <span className="eyebrow">{artwork.category}</span>
    20 |           <button
    21 |             className={`favorite-button${favorite ? ' is-favorite' : ''}`}
    22 |             type="button"
    23 |             onClick={() => toggleFavorite(artwork.id)}
    24 |             aria-label={favorite ? `Remove ${artwork.title} from my collection` : `Add ${artwork.title} to my collection`}
    25 |             aria-pressed={favorite}
    26 |           >
    27 |             <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M12 21s-7.5-4.7-9.5-9C1 8.5 3 5 6.5 5c2 0 3.5 1 4.5 2.7C12 6 13.5 5 15.5 5 19 5 21 8.5 20.5 12c-1 4.3-8.5 9-8.5 9Z" /></svg>
    28 |           </button>
    29 |         </div>
    30 |         <h3><Link to={`/works/${artwork.id}`}>{artwork.title}</Link></h3>
    31 |         <p className="artwork-credit">{artist?.name} · {artwork.date}</p>
    32 |         {variant !== 'compact' && <p className="artwork-card-description">{artwork.description}</p>}
    33 |       </div>
    34 |     </article>
    35 |   );
    36 | }

FILE: src/components/Header.tsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
     1 | import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
     2 | import { Link, NavLink, useLocation, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
     3 | import { useFavorites } from '../context/FavoritesContext';
     4 | 
     5 | const primaryLinks = [
     6 |   { to: '/exhibitions', label: 'Exhibitions' },
     7 |   { to: '/collections', label: 'Collections' },
     8 |   { to: '/artists', label: 'Artists' },
     9 |   { to: '/timeline', label: 'Timeline' },
    10 |   { to: '/about', label: 'About' },
    11 | ];
    12 | 
    13 | export function Header() {
    14 |   const [menuOpen, setMenuOpen] = useState(false);
    15 |   const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
    16 |   const { pathname } = useLocation();
    17 |   const navigate = useNavigate();
    18 |   const { favorites } = useFavorites();
    19 | 
    20 |   useEffect(() => setMenuOpen(false), [pathname]);
    21 | 
    22 |   useEffect(() => {
    23 |     document.body.classList.toggle('menu-is-open', menuOpen);
    24 |     return () => document.body.classList.remove('menu-is-open');
    25 |   }, [menuOpen]);
    26 | 
    27 |   const submitSearch = (event: React.FormEvent) => {
    28 |     event.preventDefault();
    29 |     const value = query.trim();
    30 |     navigate(value ? `/search?q=${encodeURIComponent(value)}` : '/search');
    31 |     setMenuOpen(false);
    32 |   };
    33 | 
    34 |   return (
    35 |     <header className="site-header">
    36 |       <div className="header-inner">
    37 |         <Link className="brand" to="/" aria-label="The Meridian Archive home">
    38 |           <span className="brand-mark" aria-hidden="true">M</span>
    39 |           <span>
    40 |             <strong>Meridian</strong>
    41 |             <small>Archive</small>
    42 |           </span>
    43 |         </Link>
    44 | 
    45 |         <nav className={`primary-nav${menuOpen ? ' is-open' : ''}`} aria-label="Primary navigation">
    46 |           <div className="mobile-nav-top">
    47 |             <span>Explore the archive</span>
    48 |             <button
    49 |               className="icon-button menu-close"
    50 |               type="button"
    51 |               onClick={() => setMenuOpen(false)}
    52 |               aria-label="Close navigation"
    53 |             >
    54 |               ×
    55 |             </button>
    56 |           </div>
    57 |           {primaryLinks.map((link) => (
    58 |             <NavLink key={link.to} to={link.to}>
    59 |               {link.label}
    60 |             </NavLink>
    61 |           ))}
    62 |           <Link className="mobile-favorites-link" to="/favorites">
    63 |             My collection <span>{favorites.length}</span>
    64 |           </Link>
    65 |         </nav>
    66 | 
    67 |         <div className="header-actions">
    68 |           <form className="header-search" role="search" onSubmit={submitSearch}>
    69 |             <label className="sr-only" htmlFor="site-search">Search the museum</label>
    70 |             <input
    71 |               id="site-search"
    72 |               value={query}
    73 |               onChange={(event) => setQuery(event.target.value)}
    74 |               placeholder="Search the archive"
    75 |               type="search"
    76 |             />
    77 |             <button type="submit" aria-label="Submit search">
    78 |               <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="11" cy="11" r="6.5" /><path d="m16 16 4 4" /></svg>
    79 |             </button>
    80 |           </form>
    81 |           <Link className="favorites-link" to="/favorites" aria-label={`My collection, ${favorites.length} items`}>
    82 |             <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M12 21s-7.5-4.7-9.5-9C1 8.5 3 5 6.5 5c2 0 3.5 1 4.5 2.7C12 6 13.5 5 15.5 5 19 5 21 8.5 20.5 12c-1 4.3-8.5 9-8.5 9Z" /></svg>
    83 |             <span>{favorites.length}</span>
    84 |           </Link>
    85 |           <button
    86 |             className="icon-button menu-toggle"
    87 |             type="button"
    88 |             onClick={() => setMenuOpen((open) => !open)}
    89 |             aria-expanded={menuOpen}
    90 |             aria-controls="primary-navigation"
    91 |             aria-label={menuOpen ? 'Close navigation' : 'Open navigation'}
    92 |           >
    93 |             {menuOpen ? '×' : <span><i /><i /><i /></span>}
    94 |           </button>
    95 |         </div>
    96 |       </div>
    97 |     </header>
    98 |   );
    99 | }

================================================================================
FILE: src/context/FavoritesContext.tsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
     1 | import { createContext, useContext, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
     2 | 
     3 | const storageKey = 'meridian-favorites';
     4 | 
     5 | type FavoritesContextValue = {
     6 |   favorites: string[];
     7 |   isFavorite: (artworkId: string) => boolean;
     8 |   toggleFavorite: (artworkId: string) => void;
     9 | };
    10 | 
    11 | const FavoritesContext = createContext<FavoritesContextValue | null>(null);
    12 | 
    13 | export function FavoritesProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
    14 |   const [favorites, setFavorites] = useState<string[]>(() => {
    15 |     try {
    16 |       const stored = window.localStorage.getItem(storageKey);
    17 |       return stored ? (JSON.parse(stored) as string[]) : [];
    18 |     } catch {
    19 |       return [];
    20 |     }
    21 |   });
    22 | 
    23 |   useEffect(() => {
    24 |     window.localStorage.setItem(storageKey, JSON.stringify(favorites));
    25 |   }, [favorites]);
    26 | 
    27 |   const value = useMemo<FavoritesContextValue>(
    28 |     () => ({
    29 |       favorites,
    30 |       isFavorite: (artworkId) => favorites.includes(artworkId),
    31 |       toggleFavorite: (artworkId) =>
    32 |         setFavorites((current) =>
    33 |           current.includes(artworkId)
    34 |             ? current.filter((id) => id !== artworkId)
    35 |             : [...current, artworkId],
    36 |         ),
    37 |     }),
    38 |     [favorites],
    39 |   );
    40 | 
    41 |   return (
    42 |     <FavoritesContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</FavoritesContext.Provider>
    43 |   );
    44 | }
    45 | 
    46 | export function useFavorites() {
    47 |   const context = useContext(FavoritesContext);
    48 |   if (!context) {
    49 |     throw new Error('useFavorites must be used within FavoritesProvider');
    50 |   }
    51 |   return context;
    52 | }

FILE: src/data/museumData.ts
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
     1 | import type { Artist, Artwork, Collection, Exhibition, MuseumData, TimelineEvent } from '../types';
     2 | 
     3 | const image = (file: string, width = 1280) => {
     4 |   const filename = file.slice(file.lastIndexOf('/') + 1);
     5 |   const title = decodeURIComponent(filename).replaceAll('_', ' ');
     6 |   return `https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/Special:Redirect/file/${encodeURIComponent(title)}?width=${width}`;
     7 | };

FILE: src/hooks/usePageEffects.ts
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
     1 | import { useEffect } from 'react';
     2 | import { useLocation } from 'react-router-dom';
     3 | 
     4 | export function usePageEffects() {
     5 |   const { pathname } = useLocation();
     6 | 
     7 |   useEffect(() => {
     8 |     window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'instant' });
     9 |   }, [pathname]);
    10 | 
    11 |   useEffect(() => {
    12 |     const elements = document.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[data-reveal]');
    13 |     const observer = new IntersectionObserver(
    14 |       (entries) => {
    15 |         entries.forEach((entry) => {
    16 |           if (entry.isIntersecting) {
    17 |             entry.target.classList.add('is-visible');
    18 |             observer.unobserve(entry.target);
    19 |           }
    20 |         });
    21 |       },
    22 |       { threshold: 0.12, rootMargin: '0px 0px -40px' },
    23 |     );
    24 | 
    25 |     elements.forEach((element) => observer.observe(element));
    26 |     return () => observer.disconnect();
    27 |   }, [pathname]);
    28 | }

     1 | import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';

     4 | export function AboutPage() {
     5 |   return (
     6 |     <>
     7 |       <PageHero eyebrow="Our museum" title="About the Meridian Archive" description="Our museum" image="https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/Special:Redirect/file/Van%20Gogh%20-%20Starry%20Night%20-%20Google%20Art%20Project.jpg?width=1280" imageAlt="A painted night sky" />

     9 |         <div className="section-inner mission-statement"><p className="eyebrow">Our mission</p><h2>To make looking feel like a place you can enter.</h2><p>The Meridian Archive is a fictional virtual museum designed as a working model for digital interpretation. We bring together public-domain-inspired imagery, structured collection data, and editorial storytelling so that a visit can feel as considered as a walk through a physical gallery.</p></div>
    10 |       </section>
    11 |       <section className="section section-tinted">
    12 |         <div className="section-inner about-story-grid">
    13 |           <div><p className="eyebrow">Our story</p><h2>A museum without walls</h2></div>
    14 |           <div><p>The archive began as a simple question: what if a museum could keep the quiet, the pacing, and the sense of discovery of a gallery while becoming available to anyone with an internet connection? The result is a responsive, accessible website where every route is a room and every object record is an invitation to look more closely.</p><p>Our content is deliberately fictional and clearly identified as such. Image sources are drawn from public-domain and open-access collections, while the data model is designed to be replaced by real institutional records.</p></div>
    15 |         </div>
    16 |       </section>
    17 |       <section className="section">
    18 |         <div className="section-inner about-columns">
    19 |           <div><p className="eyebrow">Curatorial approach</p><h2>Context before spectacle.</h2><p>We favor slow looking over endless scrolling. Each exhibition is built around a question, each object record includes material and provenance fields, and each page leaves space for uncertainty.</p></div>
    20 |           <div><p className="eyebrow">Virtual museum concept</p><h2>Designed for the way people wander.</h2><p>Search, favorites, filters, and timeline navigation work together without turning the museum into a database. The interface is a guide, not a gatekeeper.</p></div>
    21 |           <div><p className="eyebrow">Access &amp; care</p><h2>Built to be usable by more people.</h2><p>Semantic landmarks, keyboard-operable controls, descriptive alt text, responsive layouts, and reduced-motion support are part of the exhibition design from the beginning.</p></div>

FILE: src/pages/ArtistDetailPage.tsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
     1 | import { useMemo } from 'react';
     2 | import { Link, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';
     3 | import { ArtworkCard } from '../components/ArtworkCard';
     4 | import { Breadcrumbs } from '../components/Breadcrumbs';
     5 | import { EmptyState, SectionHeading } from '../components/UI';
     6 | import { artists, artworks } from '../data/museumData';
     7 | 
     8 | export function ArtistDetailPage() {
     9 |   const { artistId = '' } = useParams();
    10 |   const artist = artists.find((item) => item.id === artistId);
    11 |   const works = useMemo(() => artist ? artworks.filter((work) => work.artistId === artist.id) : [], [artist]);
    12 | 
    13 |   if (!artist) {
    14 |     return <EmptyState title="Creator not found" description="This profile may be between installations. Return to the directory to keep exploring." action={<Link className="button button-dark" to="/artists">View artists</Link>} />;
    15 |   }
    16 | 
    17 |   return (
    18 |     <>
    19 |       <Breadcrumbs items={[{ label: 'Artists', to: '/artists' }, { label: artist.name }]} />
    20 |       <section className="artist-detail-hero">
    21 |         <div className="artist-detail-hero-image"><img src={artist.portrait} alt={`Portrait of ${artist.name}`} /></div>
    22 |         <div className="artist-detail-hero-copy">
    23 |           <p className="eyebrow">{artist.movement}</p>
    24 |           <h1>{artist.name}</h1>
    25 |           <p className="artist-detail-lifespan">{artist.birth}–{artist.death} · {artist.nationality}</p>
    26 |           <p>{artist.bio}</p>
    27 |           <div className="artist-detail-facts"><span><small>Nationality</small>{artist.nationality}</span><span><small>Movement</small>{artist.movement}</span><span><small>Works here</small>{works.length}</span></div>
    28 |         </div>
    29 |       </section>
    30 |       <section className="section section-tinted">
    31 |         <div className="section-inner">
    32 |           <SectionHeading eyebrow="Selected works" title="A closer look" description="These works are connected to the creator's profile and can be explored as individual object records." />
    33 |           {works.length > 0 ? <div className="artwork-grid">{works.map((work, index) => <ArtworkCard key={work.id} artwork={work} variant={index === 0 ? 'feature' : 'standard'} />)}</div> : <EmptyState title="No works on view" description="This creator profile is ready for new collection records." />}
    34 |         </div>
    35 |       </section>
    36 |       <section className="section">
    37 |         <div className="section-inner artist-bio-panel">
    38 |           <div><p className="eyebrow">Biography</p><h2>Looking as a form of practice</h2></div>
    39 |           <div><p>{artist.bio}</p><p>The Meridian Archive uses creator biographies as interpretive starting points rather than final answers. Each profile leaves room for new research, alternative voices, and the object itself.</p></div>
    40 |         </div>
    41 |       </section>
    42 |     </>
    43 |   );
    44 | }

FILE: src/pages/ArtistsPage.tsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
     1 | import { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
     2 | import { ArtistCard } from '../components/ArtistCard';
     3 | import { EmptyState, PageHero, SectionHeading } from '../components/UI';
     4 | import { artists } from '../data/museumData';
     5 | 
     6 | export function ArtistsPage() {
     7 |   const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
     8 |   const [movement, setMovement] = useState('All');
     9 |   const movements = ['All', ...Array.from(new Set(artists.map((artist) => artist.movement)))];
    10 | 
    11 |   const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    12 |     const normalized = query.trim().toLowerCase();
    13 |     return artists.filter((artist) => {
    14 |       const matchesQuery = !normalized || `${artist.name} ${artist.bio} ${artist.nationality} ${artist.movement}`.toLowerCase().includes(normalized);
    15 |       return matchesQuery && (movement === 'All' || artist.movement === movement);
    16 |     });
    17 |   }, [query, movement]);
    18 | 
    19 |   return (
    20 |     <>
    21 |       <PageHero eyebrow="Creator directory" title="The hands behind the hours" description="Meet the artists, workshops, and makers whose decisions continue to shape the way we see." image="https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/Special:Redirect/file/Vincent%20van%20Gogh%20-%20Self-Portrait%20-%20Google%20Art%20Project.jpg?width=1280" imageAlt="Portrait of Vincent van Gogh" />
    22 |       <section className="section">
    23 |         <div className="section-inner">
    24 |           <div className="section-heading-row">
    25 |             <SectionHeading eyebrow="Directory" title="Search the makers" description="Browse by name, movement, nationality, or a detail from a biography." />
    26 |             <span className="result-count">{filtered.length} {filtered.length === 1 ? 'creator' : 'creators'}</span>
    27 |           </div>
    28 |           <div className="filter-bar">
    29 |             <label className="filter-search">
    30 |               <span className="sr-only">Search artists</span>
    31 |               <input value={query} onChange={(event) => setQuery(event.target.value)} placeholder="Search artists or movements" type="search" />
    32 |             </label>
    33 |             <label>
    34 |               <span>Movement</span>
    35 |               <select value={movement} onChange={(event) => setMovement(event.target.value)}>{movements.map((item) => <option key={item}>{item}</option>)}</select>
    36 |             </label>
    37 |             {(query || movement !== 'All') && <button className="filter-clear" type="button" onClick={() => { setQuery(''); setMovement('All'); }}>Clear filters</button>}
    38 |           </div>
    39 |           {filtered.length > 0 ? (
    40 |             <div className="artist-grid">{filtered.map((artist) => <ArtistCard key={artist.id} artist={artist} />)}</div>
    41 |           ) : <EmptyState title="No creators found" description="Try a different name or broaden the movement filter to continue through the directory." action={<button className="button button-dark" type="button" onClick={() => { setQuery(''); setMovement('All'); }}>Reset the directory</button>} />}
    42 |         </div>
    43 |       </section>
    44 |     </>
    45 |   );
    46 | }

     1 | import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
     2 | import { Link, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';
     3 | import { ArtworkCard } from '../components/ArtworkCard';
     4 | import { Breadcrumbs } from '../components/Breadcrumbs';
     5 | import { EmptyState } from '../components/UI';
     6 | import { useFavorites } from '../context/FavoritesContext';
     7 | import { artworks, getArtist } from '../data/museumData';
     8 | 
     9 | export function ArtworkDetailPage() {
    10 |   const { artworkId = '' } = useParams();
    11 |   const artwork = artworks.find((item) => item.id === artworkId);
    12 |   const artist = artwork ? getArtist(artwork.artistId) : undefined;
    13 |   const { isFavorite, toggleFavorite } = useFavorites();
    14 |   const [zoomOpen, setZoomOpen] = useState(false);
    15 |   const [zoom, setZoom] = useState(1);
    16 | 
    17 |   useEffect(() => {
    18 |     if (!zoomOpen) setZoom(1);
    19 |   }, [zoomOpen]);
    20 | 
    21 |   useEffect(() => {
    22 |     const onKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
    23 |       if (event.key === 'Escape') setZoomOpen(false);
    24 |     };
    25 |     window.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    26 |     return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    27 |   }, []);
    28 | 
    29 |   if (!artwork || !artist) {
    30 |     return <EmptyState title="Object not found" description="This record may be between installations. Return to the archive to keep exploring." action={<Link className="button button-dark" to="/collections">Browse collections</Link>} />;
    31 |   }
    32 | 
    33 |   const related = artworks.filter((item) => item.id !== artwork.id && (item.category === artwork.category || item.collectionIds.some((id) => artwork.collectionIds.includes(id)))).slice(0, 3);
    34 |   const favorite = isFavorite(artwork.id);
    35 |

    37 |     <>
    38 |       <Breadcrumbs items={[{ label: 'Collections', to: '/collections' }, { label: artwork.title }]} />

    43 |             <span className="viewer-hint">Click to enlarge</span>
    44 |           </button>
    45 |           <div className="zoom-controls" aria-label="Image viewer controls">
    46 |             <button type="button" onClick={() => setZoomOpen(true)} aria-label="Enlarge image">+</button>
    47 |             <span>View details</span>
    48 |           </div>
    49 |         </div>

    53 |           <p className="artwork-artist-line"><Link to={`/artists/${artist.id}`}>{artist.name}</Link></p>

    63 |             {favorite ? 'Saved to my collection' : 'Save to my collection'}
    64 |           </button>

    77 |           </div>
    78 |         </div>
    79 |       </section>
    80 | 
    81 |       <section className="section section-tinted">
    82 |         <div className="section-inner provenance-panel">

    84 |             <p className="eyebrow">Collection record</p>

    86 |           </div>

    88 |             <p>{artwork.provenance}</p>

   121 |             <button className="image-modal-close" type="button" onClick={() => setZoomOpen(false)} aria-label="Close image viewer">×</button>

   124 |           </div>
   125 |           <div className="image-modal-controls" onClick={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}>
   126 |             <button type="button" onClick={() => setZoom((value) => Math.max(0.7, value - 0.2))} aria-label="Zoom out">−</button>
   127 |             <span>{Math.round(zoom * 100)}%</span>
   128 |             <button type="button" onClick={() => setZoom((value) => Math.min(2.2, value + 0.2))} aria-label="Zoom in">+</button>
   129 |           </div>

FILE: src/pages/CollectionsPage.tsx

     1 | import { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
     2 | import { ArtworkCard } from '../components/ArtworkCard';
     3 | import { EmptyState, PageHero, SectionHeading } from '../components/UI';
     4 | import { artworks, collections } from '../data/museumData';
     5 | 
     6 | export function CollectionsPage() {
     7 |   const [activeCollection, setActiveCollection] = useState<string>('all');
     8 |   const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
     9 |   const [category, setCategory] = useState('All');
    10 | 
    11 |   const visibleWorks = useMemo(() => {
    12 |     const normalized = query.trim().toLowerCase();
    13 |     return artworks.filter((work) => {
    14 |       const matchesCollection = activeCollection === 'all' || work.collectionIds.includes(activeCollection);
    15 |       const matchesCategory = category === 'All' || work.category === category;
    16 |       const matchesQuery = !normalized || `${work.title} ${work.medium} ${work.location} ${work.tags.join(' ')}`.toLowerCase().includes(normalized);
    17 |       return matchesCollection && matchesCategory && matchesQuery;
    18 |     });
    19 |   }, [activeCollection, category, query]);
    20 | 
    21 |   const categories = ['All', ...Array.from(new Set(artworks.map((work) => work.category)))];
    22 | 
    23 |   return (
    24 |     <>
    25 |       <PageHero eyebrow="The archive" title="Collections for every kind of looking" description="Browse by material, era, or medium. Every object record is designed to be a doorway into a larger story." image="https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/Special:Redirect/file/KellsFol032vChristEnthroned.jpg?width=1280" imageAlt="An illuminated manuscript page with interlaced forms" />
    26 |       <section className="section">
    27 |         <div className="section-inner">
    28 |           <SectionHeading eyebrow="Browse the archive" title="Start with a collection" description="Choose a collection below, then refine the works on view with search and category filters." />
    29 |           <div className="collection-grid collection-grid-four">
    30 |             {collections.map((collection) => (
    31 |               <button className={`collection-tile${activeCollection === collection.id ? ' is-active' : ''}`} key={collection.id} type="button" onClick={() => setActiveCollection((current) => current === collection.id ? 'all' : collection.id)}>
    32 |                 <img src={collection.image} alt="" loading="lazy" />
    33 |                 <span className="collection-tile-overlay"><strong>{collection.name}</strong><small>{collection.workIds.length} works</small></span>
    34 |               </button>
    35 |             ))}
    36 |           </div>
    37 |         </div>
    38 |       </section>
    39 | 
    40 |       <section className="section section-tinted collection-browser" id={activeCollection === 'all' ? undefined : activeCollection}>
    41 |         <div className="section-inner">
    42 |           <div className="section-heading-row">
    43 |             <div>
    44 |               <p className="eyebrow">{activeCollection === 'all' ? 'All collections' : collections.find((collection) => collection.id === activeCollection)?.name}</p>
    45 |               <h2>{visibleWorks.length} {visibleWorks.length === 1 ? 'object' : 'objects'} on view</h2>
    46 |             </div>
    47 |             <span className="result-count">Archive view</span>
    48 |           </div>
    49 |           <div className="filter-bar collection-filter-bar">
    50 |             <label className="filter-search">
    51 |               <span className="sr-only">Search works</span>
    52 |               <input value={query} onChange={(event) => setQuery(event.target.value)} placeholder="Search works, materials, or locations" type="search" />
    53 |             </label>
    54 |             <label>
    55 |               <span>Category</span>
    56 |               <select value={category} onChange={(event) => setCategory(event.target.value)}>{categories.map((item) => <option key={item}>{item}</option>)}</select>
    57 |             </label>
    58 |             {activeCollection !== 'all' && <button className="filter-clear" type="button" onClick={() => setActiveCollection('all')}>Show all collections</button>}
    59 |           </div>
    60 |           {visibleWorks.length > 0 ? (
    61 |             <div className="artwork-grid">{visibleWorks.map((work, index) => <ArtworkCard key={work.id} artwork={work} variant={index === 0 ? 'feature' : 'standard'} />)}</div>
    62 |           ) : <EmptyState title="No objects found" description="Try a different search or choose another collection to continue your visit." action={<button className="button button-dark" type="button" onClick={() => { setQuery(''); setCategory('All'); setActiveCollection('all'); }}>Reset the archive</button>} />}
    63 |         </div>
    64 |       </section>
    65 | 
    66 |       <section className="section collection-note">
    67 |         <div className="section-inner collection-note-grid">
    68 |           <div><p className="eyebrow">A living archive</p><h2>Built to be replaced, ready to be explored.</h2></div>
    69 |           <p>The collection data lives in one structured source, so a real institution can swap in its own records, image rights, and collection-management links without rebuilding the visitor experience.</p>
    70 |         </div>
    71 |       </section>
    72 |     </>
    73 |   );
    74 | }

     1 | import { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
     2 | import { Link, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';

     8 | import type { Artwork } from '../types';
     9 | 
    10 | export function ExhibitionDetailPage() {
    11 |   const { slug = '' } = useParams();
    12 |   const exhibition = exhibitions.find((item) => item.slug === slug);
    13 |   const [activeWork, setActiveWork] = useState(artworks.find((work) => work.id === exhibition?.workIds[0])?.id ?? '');
    14 |   const activeArtwork = artworks.find((work) => work.id === activeWork);
    15 | 
    16 |   const relatedExhibitions = useMemo(() => exhibition?.relatedExhibitionIds.map((id) => exhibitions.find((item) => item.id === id)).filter(Boolean) ?? [], [exhibition]);
    17 |   const relatedArtists = useMemo(() => exhibition?.relatedArtistIds.map((id) => artists.find((item) => item.id === id)).filter(Boolean) ?? [], [exhibition]);
    18 |   const featuredWorks = useMemo<Artwork[]>(() => exhibition?.workIds.map((id) => artworks.find((work) => work.id === id)).filter((work): work is Artwork => Boolean(work)) ?? [], [exhibition]);
    19 | 
    20 |   if (!exhibition) {
    21 |     return <EmptyState title="Exhibition not found" description="This gallery may be between installations. Return to the exhibition program to keep exploring." action={<Link className="button button-dark" to="/exhibitions">View exhibitions</Link>} />;
    22 |   }
    23 | 
    24 |   return (
    25 |     <>
    26 |       <Breadcrumbs items={[{ label: 'Exhibitions', to: '/exhibitions' }, { label: exhibition.title }]} />
    27 |       <section className="exhibition-intro">
    28 |         <div className="exhibition-intro-copy">
    29 |           <p className="eyebrow">{exhibition.category} · {exhibition.period}</p>

    37 |           </div>
    38 |         </div>

FILE: src/pages/ExhibitionsPage.tsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
     1 | import { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
     2 | import { ExhibitionCard } from '../components/ExhibitionCard';
     3 | import { PageHero, SectionHeading, EmptyState } from '../components/UI';
     4 | import { exhibitions } from '../data/museumData';
     5 | 
     6 | const categories = ['All', ...Array.from(new Set(exhibitions.map((item) => item.category)))];
     7 | const periods = ['All', ...Array.from(new Set(exhibitions.map((item) => item.period)))];
     8 | 
     9 | export function ExhibitionsPage() {
    10 |   const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
    11 |   const [category, setCategory] = useState('All');
    12 |   const [period, setPeriod] = useState('All');
    13 |   const [sort, setSort] = useState('featured');
    14 | 
    15 |   const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    16 |     const normalized = query.trim().toLowerCase();
    17 |     return exhibitions
    18 |       .filter((exhibition) => {
    19 |         const matchesQuery = !normalized || `${exhibition.title} ${exhibition.dek} ${exhibition.curator} ${exhibition.description}`.toLowerCase().includes(normalized);
    20 |         const matchesCategory = category === 'All' || exhibition.category === category;
    21 |         const matchesPeriod = period === 'All' || exhibition.period === period;
    22 |         return matchesQuery && matchesCategory && matchesPeriod;
    23 |       })
    24 |       .sort((a, b) => {
    25 |         if (sort === 'title') return a.title.localeCompare(b.title);
    26 |         if (sort === 'newest') return b.period.localeCompare(a.period);
    27 |         if (sort === 'works') return b.workIds.length - a.workIds.length;
    28 |         return Number(b.featured) - Number(a.featured);
    29 |       });
    30 |   }, [query, category, period, sort]);
    31 | 
    32 |   return (
    33 |     <>
    34 |       <PageHero eyebrow="Exhibition program" title="Rooms for every kind of curiosity" description="Browse the current and upcoming exhibitions, then choose your own path through the archive." image="https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/Special:Redirect/file/Van%20Gogh%20-%20Starry%20Night%20-%20Google%20Art%20Project.jpg?width=1280" imageAlt="A painted night sky above a quiet village" />
    35 |       <section className="section">
    36 |         <div className="section-inner">
    37 |           <div className="section-heading-row">
    38 |             <SectionHeading eyebrow="The program" title="Find an exhibition" description="Search by title or curator, filter by period and category, and sort the galleries to suit your mood." />
    39 |             <span className="result-count">{filtered.length} {filtered.length === 1 ? 'exhibition' : 'exhibitions'}</span>
    40 |           </div>
    41 |           <div className="filter-bar" aria-label="Exhibition filters">
    42 |             <label className="filter-search">
    43 |               <span className="sr-only">Search exhibitions</span>
    44 |               <input value={query} onChange={(event) => setQuery(event.target.value)} placeholder="Search exhibitions or curators" type="search" />
    45 |             </label>
    46 |             <label>
    47 |               <span>Category</span>
    48 |               <select value={category} onChange={(event) => setCategory(event.target.value)}>
    49 |                 {categories.map((item) => <option key={item}>{item}</option>)}
    50 |               </select>
    51 |             </label>
    52 |             <label>
    53 |               <span>Period</span>
    54 |               <select value={period} onChange={(event) => setPeriod(event.target.value)}>
    55 |                 {periods.map((item) => <option key={item}>{item}</option>)}
    56 |               </select>
    57 |             </label>
    58 |             <label>
    59 |               <span>Sort by</span>
    60 |               <select value={sort} onChange={(event) => setSort(event.target.value)}>
    61 |                 <option value="featured">Featured first</option>
    62 |                 <option value="title">Title A–Z</option>
    63 |                 <option value="newest">Period</option>
    64 |                 <option value="works">Most works</option>
    65 |               </select>
    66 |             </label>
    67 |             {(query || category !== 'All' || period !== 'All') && <button className="filter-clear" type="button" onClick={() => { setQuery(''); setCategory('All'); setPeriod('All'); }}>Clear filters</button>}
    68 |           </div>
    69 |           {filtered.length > 0 ? (
    70 |             <div className="exhibition-grid exhibition-grid-results">
    71 |               {filtered.map((exhibition, index) => <ExhibitionCard key={exhibition.id} exhibition={exhibition} featured={index === 0 && query === '' && category === 'All' && period === 'All'} />)}
    72 |             </div>
    73 |           ) : <EmptyState title="No exhibitions found" description="Try a different search term or broaden your filters to continue exploring." action={<button className="button button-dark" type="button" onClick={() => { setQuery(''); setCategory('All'); setPeriod('All'); }}>Reset the gallery</button>} />}
    74 |         </div>
    75 |       </section>
    76 |     </>
    77 |   );
    78 | }

FILE: src/pages/SearchPage.tsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
     1 | import { useMemo } from 'react';
     2 | import { Link, useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom';
     3 | import { ArtworkCard } from '../components/ArtworkCard';
     4 | import { ExhibitionCard } from '../components/ExhibitionCard';
     5 | import { ArtistCard } from '../components/ArtistCard';
     6 | import { CollectionCard } from '../components/CollectionCard';
     7 | import { EmptyState, PageHero, SectionHeading } from '../components/UI';
     8 | import { artists, artworks, collections, exhibitions, timeline } from '../data/museumData';
     9 | 
    10 | export function SearchPage() {
    11 |   const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();
    12 |   const query = (searchParams.get('q') ?? '').trim();
    13 |   const normalized = query.toLowerCase();
    14 | 
    15 |   const results = useMemo(() => {
    16 |     if (!normalized) return null;
    17 |     const artworkResults = artworks.filter((item) => `${item.title} ${item.medium} ${item.location} ${item.tags.join(' ')} ${item.description}`.toLowerCase().includes(normalized));
    18 |     const artistResults = artists.filter((item) => `${item.name} ${item.bio} ${item.nationality} ${item.movement}`.toLowerCase().includes(normalized));
    19 |     const exhibitionResults = exhibitions.filter((item) => `${item.title} ${item.dek} ${item.description} ${item.curator}`.toLowerCase().includes(normalized));
    20 |     const collectionResults = collections.filter((item) => `${item.name} ${item.description} ${item.category}`.toLowerCase().includes(normalized));
    21 |     const eventResults = timeline.filter((item) => `${item.title} ${item.description} ${item.period}`.toLowerCase().includes(normalized));
    22 |     return { artworkResults, artistResults, exhibitionResults, collectionResults, eventResults };
    23 |   }, [normalized]);
    24 | 
    25 |   const total = results ? Object.values(results).reduce((sum, items) => sum + items.length, 0) : 0;
    26 | 
    27 |   return (
    28 |     <>
    29 |       <PageHero eyebrow="Global search" title="Search the whole archive" description="Find artworks, artifacts, artists, exhibitions, collections, and historical moments in one place." image="https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/Special:Redirect/file/KellsFol032vChristEnthroned.jpg?width=1280" imageAlt="A detailed manuscript page" />
    30 |       <section className="section search-page">
    31 |         <div className="section-inner">
    32 |           <SectionHeading eyebrow="Search results" title={query ? `Results for “${query}”` : 'Begin with a word'} description={query ? `${total} ${total === 1 ? 'result' : 'results'} across the archive` : 'Type a name, material, place, movement, or idea into the search field above.'} />
    33 |           {query && results && total > 0 ? (
    34 |             <div className="search-results">
    35 |               {results.artworkResults.length > 0 && <SearchGroup title="Works" count={results.artworkResults.length}><div className="artwork-grid">{results.artworkResults.map((item) => <ArtworkCard key={item.id} artwork={item} variant="compact" />)}</div></SearchGroup>}
    36 |               {results.artistResults.length > 0 && <SearchGroup title="Artists" count={results.artistResults.length}><div className="artist-grid">{results.artistResults.map((item) => <ArtistCard key={item.id} artist={item} />)}</div></SearchGroup>}
    37 |               {results.exhibitionResults.length > 0 && <SearchGroup title="Exhibitions" count={results.exhibitionResults.length}><div className="exhibition-grid">{results.exhibitionResults.map((item) => <ExhibitionCard key={item.id} exhibition={item} />)}</div></SearchGroup>}
    38 |               {results.collectionResults.length > 0 && <SearchGroup title="Collections" count={results.collectionResults.length}><div className="collection-grid">{results.collectionResults.map((item) => <CollectionCard key={item.id} collection={item} />)}</div></SearchGroup>}
    39 |               {results.eventResults.length > 0 && <SearchGroup title="Historical moments" count={results.eventResults.length}><div className="search-event-list">{results.eventResults.map((item) => <Link key={item.id} to={item.artworkId ? `/works/${item.artworkId}` : '/timeline'}><span>{item.year}</span><strong>{item.title}</strong><small>{item.description}</small></Link>)}</div></SearchGroup>}
    40 |             </div>
    41 |           ) : query ? <EmptyState title="No results for this search" description="Check the spelling or try a broader term such as “light”, “Egypt”, or “portrait”." action={<Link className="button button-dark" to="/collections">Browse collections instead</Link>} /> : (
    42 |             <div className="search-suggestions"><h3>Popular paths through the archive</h3><div>{['light', 'portrait', 'Egypt', 'night', 'garden', 'material'].map((term) => <Link key={term} to={`/search?q=${encodeURIComponent(term)}`}>{term}</Link>)}</div></div>)}
    43 |         </div>
    44 |       </section>
    45 |     </>
    46 |   );
    47 | }
    48 | 
    49 | function SearchGroup({ title, count, children }: { title: string; count: number; children: React.ReactNode }) {
    50 |   return <section className="search-result-group"><div className="search-result-group-heading"><h2>{title}</h2><span>{count}</span></div>{children}</section>;
    51 | }

FILE: src/pages/TimelinePage.tsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
     1 | import { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
     2 | import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
     3 | import { EmptyState, PageHero, SectionHeading } from '../components/UI';
     4 | import { timeline } from '../data/museumData';
     5 | 
     6 | const kinds = ['All', 'movement', 'event', 'artist', 'work'];
     7 | 
     8 | export function TimelinePage() {
     9 |   const [activeKind, setActiveKind] = useState('All');
    10 |   const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = useState(timeline[0]?.id ?? '');
    11 |   const events = useMemo(() => timeline.filter((event) => activeKind === 'All' || event.kind === activeKind), [activeKind]);
    12 |   const selected = timeline.find((event) => event.id === selectedId) ?? events[0];
    13 | 
    14 |   return (
    15 |     <>
    16 |       <PageHero eyebrow="Historical timeline" title="A museum measured in moments" description="Follow the long conversation between materials, makers, and the worlds they inhabited." image="https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/Special:Redirect/file/KellsFol032vChristEnthroned.jpg?width=1280" imageAlt="An illuminated manuscript page" />
    17 |       <section className="section timeline-page">
    18 |         <div className="section-inner">
    19 |           <div className="section-heading-row">
    20 |             <SectionHeading eyebrow="Interactive timeline" title="Choose a moment, follow a thread" description="Filter the timeline by artistic movement, event, creator, or object, then select a moment to read its story." />
    21 |             <span className="result-count">{events.length} moments</span>
    22 |           </div>
    23 |           <div className="timeline-toolbar" role="tablist" aria-label="Timeline filters">
    24 |             {kinds.map((kind) => <button key={kind} type="button" className={activeKind === kind ? 'is-active' : ''} onClick={() => setActiveKind(kind)} role="tab" aria-selected={activeKind === kind}>{kind === 'All' ? 'All moments' : kind[0].toUpperCase() + kind.slice(1)}</button>)}
    25 |           </div>
    26 |           <div className="timeline-layout">
    27 |             <div className="timeline-track">
    28 |               {events.map((event, index) => (
    29 |                 <button className={`timeline-event${selected?.id === event.id ? ' is-active' : ''}`} key={event.id} type="button" onClick={() => setSelectedId(event.id)}>
    30 |                   <span className="timeline-event-year">{event.year}</span>
    31 |                   <span className="timeline-event-dot" aria-hidden="true" />
    32 |                   <span className="timeline-event-copy"><small>{event.kind}</small><strong>{event.title}</strong></span>
    33 |                   <span className="timeline-event-index">{String(index + 1).padStart(2, '0')}</span>
    34 |                 </button>
    35 |               ))}
    36 |             </div>
    37 |             <article className="timeline-detail">
    38 |               {selected ? (
    39 |                 <>
    40 |                   <p className="eyebrow">{selected.period} · {selected.kind}</p>
    41 |                   <h2>{selected.title}</h2>
    42 |                   <p>{selected.description}</p>
    43 |                   {selected.artworkId && <Link className="button button-outline-dark" to={`/works/${selected.artworkId}`}>View related object <span aria-hidden="true">→</span></Link>}
    44 |                 </>
    45 |               ) : <EmptyState title="Choose a moment" description="Select a point on the timeline to open its story." />}
    46 |             </article>
    47 |           </div>
    48 |           {events.length === 0 && <EmptyState title="No moments found" description="Try another timeline filter to continue through history." />}
    49 |         </div>
    50 |       </section>
    51 |     </>
    52 |   );
    53 | }

     1 | export type Artist = {
     2 |   id: string;

     4 |   birth: string;
     5 |   death: string;

     9 |   portrait: string;
    10 |   notableWorkIds: string[];
    11 | };

    19 |   period: string;

    28 |   collectionIds: string[];
    29 |   featured: boolean;
    30 |   tags: string[];
    31 | };

    48 | };

    68 |   artistId?: string;
    69 | };
